## Narration 799

[Open this narration online](https://blaghtayt-alt.github.io/Hadith-Bank/?occurrence=deenhindi-malik%3A802)

<p dir="auto"><strong>Chapter 20</strong></p>

<p><strong>Report or saying</strong> · The source attribution is explained separately for each language reading.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<p dir="rtl">[Muwatta Malik · 802]</p>

<p><strong>Muwatta Malik · 802</strong><br>source site numbering not independently canonicalized</p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنْ أَيُّوبَ بْنِ أَبِي تَمِيمَةَ السَّخْتِيَانِيِّ، عَنْ رَجُلٍ، مِنْ أَهْلِ الْبَصْرَةِ كَانَ قَدِيمًا أَنَّهُ قَالَ خَرَجْتُ إِلَى مَكَّةَ حَتَّى إِذَا كُنْتُ بِبَعْضِ الطَّرِيقِ كُسِرَتْ فَخِذِي فَأَرْسَلْتُ إِلَى مَكَّةَ وَبِهَا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عَبَّاسٍ وَعَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ وَالنَّاسُ فَلَمْ يُرَخِّصْ لِي أَحَدٌ أَنْ أَحِلَّ فَأَقَمْتُ عَلَى ذَلِكَ الْمَاءِ سَبْعَةَ أَشْهُرٍ حَتَّى أَحْلَلْتُ بِعُمْرَةٍ ‏.‏</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/802" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: historical report. Attribution: unnamed in the supplied field (unresolved). An unnamed elderly Basran reports his own injury and experience. He stays unnamed; the named persons he consulted are not made the speakers of unstated words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ayyub ibn Abi Tamima as- Sakhtayani that a very old man from Basra once said to him, &quot;I set out for Makka but on the way there I broke my thigh, so I sent a message on to Makka Abdullah ibn Abbas and Abdullah ibn Umar and the people were there, but no-one allowed me to leave ihram, and I stayed there for seven months until I left ihram by doing an umra</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 802]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/802" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>

<details>
<summary>Hindi · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="auto">अय्यूब बिन अबी तमीमा से रिवायत है के उन्होंने सुना एक शख़्स से जो बसरा का रहने वाला पुराना आदमी था, उसने कहा के मैं चला मक्का को, रास्ते में मेरा कूल्हा (hip) टूट गया तो मैंने मक्का में किसी को भेजा। वहाँ अब्दुल्लाह बिन अब्बास (रदियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) और अब्दुल्लाह बिन उमर (रदियल्लाहु अन्हुमा) और लोग भी थे, उन में से किसी ने मुझको इजाज़त न दी एहराम खोल डालने की यहाँ तक के मैं वहीं पड़ा रहा सात महीने तक। जब अच्छा हुआ तो उमराह करके एहराम खोला।</p>

<p><a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/802" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Commentary source</a></p>

</details>

<details>
<summary>Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">ایوب بن ابی تمیمہ سے روایت ہے کہ انہوں نے سنا ایک شخص سے جو بصرہ کا رہنے والا پرانا آدمی تھا اس نے کہا کہ میں چلا مکہ کو راستہ میں میرا کولہا ٹوٹ گیا تو میں نے مکہ میں کسی کو بھیجا وہاں عبداللہ بن عباس اور عبداللہ بن عمر اور لوگ بھی تھے ان میں سے کسی نے مجھ کو اجازت نہ دی احرام کھول ڈالنے کی یہاں تک کہ میں وہیں پڑا رہا سات مہینے تک جب اچھا ہوا تو عمرہ کر کے احرام کھولا۔</p>

<details>
<summary>Roman Urdu · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="ltr">Ayyub bin Abi Tamima se riwayat hai ke unhone suna ek shakhs se jo Basra ka rehne wala purana aadmi tha, usne kaha ke main chala Makkah ko, raaste mein mera koolha (hip) toot gaya toh maine Makkah mein kisi ko bheja. Wahan Abdullah bin Abbas (Radi Allahu Anhuma) aur Abdullah bin Umar (Radi Allahu Anhuma) aur log bhi the, un mein se kisi ne mujhko ijazat na di ehram khol daalne ki yahan tak ke main wahin pada raha saat mahine tak. Jab achha hua toh Umrah karke ehram khola.</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="ar">
<h3>Arabic</h3>

<p dir="auto">The attribution in this language remains under review. Its complete source text is preserved under Comments; another language is not used to supply an unnamed speaker. This language field has not yet received its own attribution and boundary review.</p>

<details>
<summary>Arabic · notes and commentary</summary>

<p dir="rtl">وَحَدَّثَنِي عَنْ مَالِكٍ، عَنِ ابْنِ شِهَابٍ، عَنْ سَالِمِ بْنِ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ، عَنْ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ، أَنَّهُ قَالَ مَنْ حُبِسَ دُونَ الْبَيْتِ بِمَرَضٍ فَإِنَّهُ لاَ يَحِلُّ حَتَّى يَطُوفَ بِالْبَيْتِ وَبَيْنَ الصَّفَا وَالْمَرْوَةِ ‏.‏</p>

<section class="language-reading" lang="en">
<h3>English</h3>

<p dir="auto">Report type: companion report. Attribution: Abdullah ibn Umar (companion). The source attributes this numbered report to the named speaker or actor; it is not relabelled as the Prophet’s words.</p>

<p dir="ltr">Yahya related to me from Malik from Ibn Shihab from Salim ibn Abdullah that Abdullah ibn Umar said, &quot;Some one who is detained by sickness before he has got to the House cannot leave ihram until he has done tawaf of the House and say between Safa and Marwa</p>

<p dir="ltr">[Muwatta Malik · 803]</p>

<p><strong>Source:</strong> <a href="https://deenhindi.com/hadith/malik/803" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Deen Hindi — Muwatta Malik</a> · Translation origin: Human</p>
